With a <a href="prepay-and-save/">bundle of PraiseCharts credits</a> to download the latest music you can quickly grab what you need for planning your worship set. Plus with a subscription to hundreds of <a href="http://www.worshiptraining.com/plans-pricing/">training resources</a> at WorshipTraining, you can train your whole team for <span>less than the cost of sending one person to one conference. </span>There are lots of options to choose from to meet your specific needs.<br/><br/><a class="pc-button wide" href="prepay-and-save" style="width: 265px; margin-top: 15px;">PraiseCharts PrePaid Credits</a>     <a class="pc-button wide" href="http://www.worshiptraining.com/plans-pricing" style="width: 265px; margin-top: 15px;">WorshipTraining Membership</a></p>

<div class="row">
<div class="large-12 columns">
<h2 class="section-title-centered dark">
<a class="section-title-centered dark" href="https://www.praisecharts.com/songs/hot" title="We have the music you need">We have the music you need</a>
</h2>
</div>
</div>
<div class="home-device-copy">
<div class="row">
<div class="small-10 medium-12 columns">
<div class="row">
<div class="small-10 medium-4 columns text-left medium-text-center end">
<h3 class="prepay-title-featured">Chord Charts</h3>
<p>Simple, readable, and clean! Our chord charts match our arrangements measure for measure.  Quickly transpose into any key and head to practice knowing all your musicians are on the same page! <a href="songs/?context=&amp;s=&amp;clear=true&amp;filters[producttypes][HasChordChart]=HasChordChart">Browse &gt;</a></p>
</div>
<div class="small-10 medium-4 columns text-left medium-text-center end">
<h3 class="prepay-title-featured">Lead Sheets</h3>
<p>Just need the melody? Our lead sheets provide all the essential details for your rhythm section and vocalists.  The top songs have harmonies written out for Soprano, Alto and Tenor so you can nail that tight, sweet sound. <a href="songs/?context=&amp;s=&amp;clear=true&amp;filters[producttypes][HasLight]=HasLight">Browse &gt;</a></p>
</div>
<div class="small-10 medium-4 columns text-left medium-text-center end">
<h3 class="prepay-title-featured">Orchestrations</h3>
<p>From Trumpet to Sax to Strings and Flutes, we've arranged the top songs as if they were recorded with a full orchestra.  You can use a few parts to color your sound, or raise up a full band of musicians who would otherwise be sitting in the pews. <a href="songs/?context=&amp;s=&amp;filters[producttypes][HasFull]=HasFull&amp;amp">Browse &gt;</a></p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div id="iphone"></div>
</div>
</div>
